    (conv_box): Conv2d(512, 42, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv_dir_cls): Conv2d(512, 12, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  )
  (point_head): None
  (roi_head): None
)
